" Full screen editor macros to aid in drawing boxes on the screen
"
" The Ctrl-keypad keys are re-defined to draw box characters
"
" Ctrl-Home is the upper left corner
" Ctrl-PgUp is the upper right corner
" Ctrl-PgDn is the lower right corner
" Ctrl-End is the lower left corner
" Ctrl-arrow keys draw a line in that direction (assumes clockwise drawing)
"
" Ctrl-5key will prompt for the size of a box to draw
"   a 2 digit number must be entered for height and width
"   use CR as 2nd digit if less than 10
"

/*
 * This is the basic IO control block. It is the minimum
 * required information for IO transfers. The buffer is
 * of variable length depending on the particular device.
 */
struct iocb {
 struct iocb *_link;
 char _unchar;
 char _dev_type;
 char _cmd_stat;
 unsigned _arg[2];
 char _flags;
 char _dev_no;
 int _index;
 int _length;
 char _buffer[0];
 };
struct msg_hdr {
 char mtype;
 unsigned marg[2];
 char mflags;
 char mdev_no;
 int mindex;
 int mlen;
 char mbuf[0];
 } ;
#define FMSG_HDR sizeof(struct msg_hdr)

 } ;

#define MODIFY_FLAGS 26
#define SET_TTY   27
#define TTY_ROOT_TID 28
/*
 * Task States
 */
#define DEAD   0
#define READY   1
#define SEND_BLOCKED 2
#define RECEIVE_BLOCKED 3
#define REPLY_BLOCKED 4
#define HELD   5
/*
 * Task flag bits
 */
#define TO_BE_HELD  0x0001
#define DOOMED   0x0002
#define ESCAPED_SHELL 0x0004
#define FETAL_SHELL  0x0008
#define CONCURRENT  0x0010
#define ADMIN   0x0020
#define FIXED   0x0040 /* Shared by code flags */
#define PRIVILEGED  0x0080 /* Shared by code flags */
#define WAIT   0x2000
#define PART_NDP_SAVE 0x4000
#define FULL_NDP_SAVE 0x8000
/*
 * Code record flag bits
 */
#define NEEDS_NDP  0x01
#define SHAREABLE  0x02
#define SET_OWNER  0x04
/*
 * System flags in sflags field
 */
#define NDP_INSTALLED 0x01
#define PASSWORDS_ON 0x02
/*
 * System port associations for hardware events
 */
#define CLOCK_EVENT  1
#define TTY_EVENT  2
#define DISK_EVENT  7
#define PRINTER_EVENT 8
/*
 * System exception bits
 */
#define EXC_HANG_UP   0x0001
#define EXC_BREAK   0x0002
#define EXC_QUIT   0x0004
#define EXC_FLOAT   0x0080
#define EXC_KILL   0x0100
#define EXC_MEM_VIOLATION 0x0400
#define EXC_PRIV_VIOLATION 0x0800
#define EXC_ALARM_CLOCK  0x2000
#define EXC_TERM   0x4000
#define EXC_DIV_ZERO  0x8000
/*
 Kernal constants
 */
#define PRIO_BITS   4
#define NUM_PRIORITIES  (1 << PRIO_BITS)
#define PRIO_MASK   (NUM_PRIORITIES - 1)
#define MIN_USER_PRIORITY PRIO_MASK
#define DEFLT_USER_PRIORITY 8
#define MAX_USER_PRIORITY 4
#define SYS_PRIORITY  1
#define BITS_PER_BLK  4
#define BLK_SIZE   (1<<BITS_PER_BLK) /* For the 8086/8088 */
#define ONE_HUNDRED_MSEC 2
#define NUM_TASKS  20
#define TID_BITS  5
#define TID_MASK  ((1 << TID_BITS) - 1)
#define NUM_PORTS  24
#define NUM_TTYS  10
#define SUPER_USER  255
#define SOH_HDR_SIZE (14-1)
#define INIT_ALLOC_SIZE 820  /* room for one file, and three ttys */
#define MIN_STACK_SIZE 1000
#define INT_STACK_SIZE 64
#define OS_SEG   0x60
#define MAGIC_MEM_SIZE 0x80
#define enable asm("sti");
#define disable asm("cli");
struct system_info {   /* Note: This struct overlays task_table[0] */
 unsigned task_base;
 char num_tasks;
 char num_events;
 char num_priorities;
 char num_ttys;
 unsigned blk_size;
 unsigned sflags;
 char slice_size;
 } ;
 
struct task_entry {
 struct task_entry *tlink;
 unsigned tid;
 unsigned blocked_on;
 char *send_msg;
 unsigned msg_length;
 unsigned data_seg;
 unsigned data_size;
 char state;
 char priority;
 char user_num;
 char task_tty;
 char slice_cnt;
 char code_index;
 unsigned tflags;
 struct task_entry *sender_queue;
 char *reply_msg;
 struct task_entry *tty_link;
 struct task_entry *father;
 struct task_entry *son;
 struct task_entry *brother;
 struct sys_mem_entry *segment_list;
 unsigned task_spare[6];
 } ;
struct event_entry {
 struct task_entry *tlink;
 unsigned tid;
 unsigned blocked_on;
 char *send_msg;
 unsigned msg_length;
 unsigned data_seg;
 unsigned data_size;  /* not currently used */
 char state;
 unsigned event_cnt;
 } ;
struct code_entry {
 unsigned code_seg;
 char link_count;
 char code_owner;
 char code_flags;
 unsigned code_size;
 unsigned init_data_size;
 unsigned const_size;
 unsigned const_chksum;
 unsigned stack_size;
 unsigned code_spare[2];
 char code_name[0];
 } ;
/*
 * NOTE: Disk load files start with a SOH (01) followed by
 *       a load_hdr_record. Following this will be data records
 *       in non-sequential order which begin with a STX (02) followed
 *       by a load_data_record_hdr. Offset and Length both have high
 *       byte first followed by low byte.
 *       Code records have a Load_type of 0, data records are type 1.
 * 
 * struct load_hdr_record {
 *  char  Code_flags;
 *  unsigned Code_size;
 *  unsigned Init_data_size;
 *  unsigned Const_size;
 *  unsigned Const_checksum;
 *  unsigned Stack_size;
 *  unsigned Code_spare;
 *  } ;
 * 
 * struct load_data_record_hdr {
 *  char Load_type;
 *  char Offset[2];
 *  char Length[2];
 *  } ;
 *
 */
struct user_memory {
 unsigned User_sp;
 unsigned User_ss;
 unsigned Exc_bits[2];
 unsigned Exc_allow[2];
 unsigned Exc_pending[2];
 unsigned Exc_fp;
 unsigned Exc_pc;
 unsigned Exc_permit[2];
 unsigned Exit_status;
 unsigned Error_status;
 unsigned My_tid;
 unsigned Dads_tid;
 char Break_count;
 char My_tty;
 unsigned Stack_base;
 char Cmd_flags;
 char My_priority;
 unsigned Stdin;
 unsigned Stdout;
 unsigned Stderr;
    unsigned Free_list;
 unsigned End_of_free;
 unsigned File_list;
 unsigned Argc;
 unsigned Argv;
 unsigned Save;
 unsigned Temp[10];
 unsigned Reserved[10];
 char Code_flags;
 char User_num;
 } ;

 unsigned dx_reg;
 unsigned cx_reg;
 unsigned bx_reg;
 unsigned bp_reg;
 unsigned ax_reg;
 unsigned pc_reg;
 unsigned cs_reg;
 unsigned flags_reg;
 unsigned ret_addr;
 } ;

/*
 * This file contains the information necessary for a subtask
 * created by the full screen editor to communicate with the
 * full screen editor via a simple message interface.
 *
 * To force the editor to drop into communication with your task
 * it is necessary to force it ready and inform it of your task
 * id. This may be accomplished by seting a user exception of
 * your task id on it.
 *
 *  set_exception(Ed_tid, 0, My_tid)
 *
 * This will unblock the editor which will then do a specific receive
 * on My_tid. Upon your death the editor will be unblocked by the system
 * and continue.
 */
/*
 * The four msg_code values to be sent to the editor.
 * The editor will reply with a return status in the msg_code field.
 * A value of zero indicates success. A value of -3 lines up with
 * the "OUT OF MEMORY" error message in appendix A of the editor
 * manual. "Unknown Command" would be a -4 and so on.
 * The command in EXEC_COMMAND is a character string made up of
 * low level editor commands (No macros are expanded).
 * For example to insert a line
 *  "i text of line to insert"
 * and to change line 27.
 *  "27c text of line to change"
 * and to write the buffer out to file "next_text"
 *  "w new_text"
 * or to simply change its name
 *  "f new_name"
 *
 * Finially, you may even force the editor to redisplay its screen
 * by sending it a DISPLAY_SCREEN message.
 */
#define EXEC_COMMAND 0 /* Execute command in msg_data   */
#define READ_CURLINE 1 /* Return current line in msg_data  */
#define READ_ABSLINE 2 /* Return line specified in msg_line */
#define READ_DATA  3 /* Return data in msg_data with the     */
       /* structure indicated in ed_data  */
#define DISPLAY_SCREEN 4 /* Force the editor to display it screen*/
struct message {
 char msg_code;
 unsigned msg_line;  /* Only used by READ_ABSLINE   */
 char msg_data[256];
 } ;
/*
 * This overlays msg_data (above) when a READ_DATA command is send.
 */
struct ed_data {
 int nladdrs;   /* Number of line address before '!' */
 int laddr1;    /* First line address     */
 int laddr2;    /* Second line address     */
       /* ALL commands should try and operate */
       /* only within laddr1 and laddr2 if they*/
       /* are specified      */
 
 int curln;    /* Current line number     */
 int curin;
 int curcol;
 int cmdin;
 int lastln;    /* Last line number      */
 int center_line;  /* Center line (ie: vc<number>)   */
 int nmarks;    /* Number of tagged lines    */
 int marker1;   /* First tagged line     */
 int marker2;   /* Second tagged line     */
 int left_margin;  /* Margin column numbers    */
 int right_margin;
 
 int screen_row;   /* Line number of top of screen   */
 int screen_col;   /* Column number of left edge of screen */
 char cursor_row;  /* Relative (0 -> 22) and    */
 char cursor_col;  /* (0 -> 79) co-ordinates of cursor  */
 char cmd_cursor_col;
 
 char cc_reg;   /* Condition code register    */
 dev_type;    /* Device type (monochrome or colour */
 
 char opt_a;    /* Editor options      */
 char opt_b;
 char state;
 char opt_d;
 char opt_f;
 char opt_i;
 char opt_j;
 char opt_l;
 char opt_m;
 char opt_n;
 char opt_s;
 char opt_t;
 char opt_w;
 
 char curfile[FNAME_SIZE + 1]; /* Current filename    */
 } ;
